{"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"call_00_tcRL7SHAboy2WNG4mhFP7196","type":"tool_result","content":"     1→# Environment for MaxDifferentGroupPairDivision\n     2→# scorer(output) -> float in [-1.0, +1.0].\n     3→\n     4→import random\n     5→from typing import Optional, List, Tuple\n     6→from Gym.environment import VerifiableEnvironment\n     7→\n     8→\n     9→class MaxDifferentGroupPairDivision_Environment(VerifiableEnvironment):\n    10→    prompt_template = (\n    11→        'You are given an array A of {N} integers: {A}\\n'\n    12→        '\\n'\n    13→        'Initially, the entire array is one single block. Let S = 0. '\n    14→        'You need to perform the following operation exactly {K} times:\\n'\n    15→        '- Choose a position `i` such that A[i] and A[i + 1] are still in the same block.\\n'\n    16→        '- Split the block into two parts: the first ends at A[i], the second starts at A[i + 1].\\n'\n    17→        '- Let `sum1` and `sum2` be the sums of the two blocks. Then, update S += sum1 × sum2.\\n'\n    18→        '\\n'\n    19→        'After {K} operations, you will have {K} + 1 blocks. '\n    20→        'Try your best to **maximize** the final value of S.\\n'\n    21→        '\\n'\n    22→        '**Output Format:** A single line containing {K} integers — the positions `i` you chose in order, '\n    23→        'separated by spaces. Put your answer inside <answer>...</answer> tags.'\n    24→    )\n    25→\n    26→    def __init__(self, **kwargs):\n    27→        super().__init__(**kwargs)\n    28→\n    29→    def _generate(self) -> None:\n    30→        \"\"\"Generate a random problem instance and precompute optimal/worst S.\"\"\"\n    31→        rng = random.Random(self.seed)\n    32→\n    33→        # Sample N and K\n    34→        N = rng.randint(5, 15)\n    35→        K = rng.randint(1, N - 1)\n    36→\n    37→        # Sample array elements\n    38→        A = [rng.randint(-10, 10) for _ in range(N)]\n    39→\n    40→        # Compute optimal (max) and worst (min) S via DP\n    41→        optimal_S, worst_S = self._compute_bounds(A, K)\n    42→\n    43→        self.parameter = {\n    44→            \"N\": N,\n    45→            \"K\": K,\n    46→            \"A\": A,\n    47→            \"optimal_S\": optimal_S,\n    48→            \"worst_S\": worst_S,\n    49→        }\n    50→\n    51→    def _compute_bounds(self, A: List[int], K: int) -> Tuple[int, int]:\n    52→        \"\"\"Compute the maximum and minimum possible S for partitioning A into K+1 blocks.\n    53→        \n    54→        S = sum_{i<j} s_i * s_j = (T^2 - sum(s_i^2)) / 2\n    55→        where T = sum(A).\n    56→        Maximizing S is equivalent to minimizing sum of squared block sums.\n    57→        Minimizing S is equivalent to maximizing sum of squared block sums.\n    58→        \"\"\"\n    59→        N = len(A)\n    60→        # Prefix sums for O(1) range sum\n    61→        pref = [0]\n    62→        for x in A:\n    63→            pref.append(pref[-1] + x)\n    64→\n    65→        def range_sum(l, r):\n    66→            return pref[r] - pref[l]\n    67→\n    68→        # DP for minimizing sum of squares (maximizes S)\n    69→        # dp_min[i][k] = min sum of squares for first i elements into k blocks\n    70→        INF = 10**18\n    71→        dp_min = [[INF] * (K + 2) for _ in range(N + 1)]\n    72→        dp_min[0][0] = 0\n    73→        for i in range(1, N + 1):\n    74→            max_k = min(i, K + 1)\n    75→            for k in range(1, max_k + 1):\n    76→                best = INF\n    77→                for j in range(k - 1, i):\n    78→                    s = range_sum(j, i)\n    79→                    val = dp_min[j][k - 1] + s * s\n    80→                    if val < best:\n    81→                        best = val\n    82→                dp_min[i][k] = best\n    83→\n    84→        # DP for maximizing sum of squares (minimizes S)\n    85→        NEG_INF = -10**18\n    86→        dp_max = [[NEG_INF] * (K + 2) for _ in range(N + 1)]\n    87→        dp_max[0][0] = 0\n    88→        for i in range(1, N + 1):\n    89→            max_k = min(i, K + 1)\n    90→            for k in range(1, max_k + 1):\n    91→                best = NEG_INF\n    92→                for j in range(k - 1, i):\n    93→                    s = range_sum(j, i)\n    94→                    val = dp_max[j][k - 1] + s * s\n    95→                    if val > best:\n    96→                        best = val\n    97→                dp_max[i][k] = best\n    98→\n    99→        T = pref[N]\n   100→        min_sum_sq = dp_min[N][K + 1]\n   101→        max_sum_sq = dp_max[N][K + 1]\n   102→\n   103→        # S = (T^2 - sum_sq) / 2\n   104→        optimal_S = (T * T - min_sum_sq) // 2  # max S\n   105→        worst_S = (T * T - max_sum_sq) // 2    # min S\n   106→\n   107→        return optimal_S, worst_S\n   108→\n   109→    def _prompt_generate(self) -> str:\n   110→        \"\"\"Format the prompt with the generated parameters.\"\"\"\n   111→        p = self.parameter\n   112→        return self.prompt_template.format(\n   113→            N=p[\"N\"],\n   114→            K=p[\"K\"],\n   115→            A=str(p[\"A\"]),\n   116→        )\n   117→\n   118→    def _process(self, answer: Optional[str]) -> Optional[List[int]]:\n   119→        \"\"\"Parse the model's answer into a list of K positions (0-indexed).\"\"\"\n   120→        if answer is None:\n   121→            return None\n   122→\n   123→        # Try to extract integers from the answer\n   124→        import re\n   125→        # Find all integers (possibly negative)\n   126→        numbers = re.findall(r'-?\\d+', answer)\n   127→        if not numbers:\n   128→            return None\n   129→\n   130→        try:\n   131→            positions = [int(x) for x in numbers]\n   132→        except ValueError:\n   133→            return None\n   134→\n   135→        # sum(s_i^2)) / 2\n        where T = sum(A).\n        Maximizing S is equivalent to minimizing sum of squared block sums.\n        Minimizing S is equivalent to maximizing sum of squared block sums.\n        \"\"\"\n        N = len(A)\n        # Prefix sums for O(1) range sum\n        pref = [0]\n        for x in A:\n            pref.append(pref[-1] + x)\n\n        def range_sum(l, r):\n            return pref[r] -        def self_generate() :\n            self.seed = seed\n            self.parameter = copy.deepcopy(parameter) if parameter is not None else {}\n\n            manual_seed(self.seed)\n            self._generate()\n        try :\n            self_generate()\n        except :\n            return False\n        return self.parameter is not None\n\n\n    @abstractmethod\n    def _generate(self) -> None :\n        \"\"\"\n        Subclasses must implement problem generation using self.seed and self.parameter.\n        \"\"\"\n        pass\n\n\n    def prompt_generator(self) -> str :\n        \"\"\"\n        Generates the prompt string for the problem.\n\n        Returns:\n            str: The formatted prompt for the problem.\n        \"\"\"\n        assert self.seed is not None and self.parameter is not None, \"generator() should be called before prompt_generator()\"\n\n        return self._prompt_generate()\n\n\n    @abstractmethod\n    def _prompt_generate(self) -> str :\n        \"\"\"\n        Subclasses must implement prompt generation using self.seed and self.parameter.\n\n        Returns:\n            str: The problem prompt.\n        \"\"\"\n        pass\n\n\n    def processor(self, output : str) -> Any :\n        \"\"\"\n        Processes the model's output to extract useful information.\n\n        Args:\n            output (str): The string output from a model.\n\n        Returns:\n            Any: Any useful information that may be used for following steps (e.g., scoring).\n        \"\"\"\n        \n        # Remove everything before the first \"Assistant:\" (if possible)\n        if \"Assistant:\" in output :\n            output = output.split(\"Assistant:\", 1)[1]\n        elif \"<|im_start|>assistant\" in output :\n            output = output.split(\"<|im_start|>assistant\", 1)[1]\n        else :\n            pass\n\n        answer_pattern = re.escape(self.answer_markers[0]) + r\"(.*?)\" + re.escape(self.answer_markers[1])\n        matches = list(re.finditer(answer_pattern, output, re.DOTALL))\n        if matches :\n            answer = matches[-1].group(1)\n        else :\n            answer = None\n        return self._process(answer)\n\n\n    @abstractmethod\n    def _process(self, answer : Optional[str]) -> Any :\n        \"\"\"\n        Subclasses must implement the processing of the answer.\n\n        Args:\n            answer (str): The model's answer. If it is None, it means the model did not provide an answer in the expected format.\n\n        Returns:\n            Any: The processed answer, which may be used for scoring.\n        \"\"\"\n        pass\n\n\n    @abstractmethod\n    def scorer(self, output : str) -> float :\n        \"\"\"\n        Computes a numeric score for the output, which should be in [-1.0, +1.0].\n\n        Args:\n            output (str): The model's output.\n\n        Returns:\n            float: The score for the given output, between -1.0 and +1.0.\n        \"\"\"\n        pass\n\n\n    def verifier(self, output : str) -> Dict[str, Union[float, int]] :\n        \"\"\"\n        Verifies the model's output.\n        \"\"\"\n        try :\n            score = self.scorer(output)\n        except :\n            score = -1.0\n        assert -1.0 <= score <= +1.0, \"Score out of bounds: score={}\\n\\nPrompt:\\n{}\".format(score, self.prompt_generator())\n        \n        eps = 1E-6\n        return dict(\n            reward = score, # [-1.0, +1.0]\n            accuracy = int(score >= self.passing_reward_threshold - eps), # 0 or 1\n            format_score = int(score >= -1.0 + eps), # 0 or 1\n        )\n\n\n    def get_config(self) -> Dict :\n        \"\"\"\n        Returns the configuration of the current problem.\n\n        Returns:\n            Dict: Dictionary with keys 'seed' and 'parameter'.\n        \"\"\"\n        return dict(seed = self.seed, parameter = self.parameter, passing_reward_threshold = self.passing_reward_threshold)\n\n\n    def set_config(self, config : Dict) -> None :\n        \"\"\"\n        Sets the configuration for the current problem.\n\n        Args:\n            config (Dict): Dictionary with 'seed' and 'parameter' keys.\n        \"\"\"\n        assert \"seed\" in config, \"seed is required in config\"\n        assert \"parameter\" in config, \"parameter is required in config\"\n        self.seed, self.parameter, self.passing_reward_threshold = config[\"seed\"], config[\"parameter\"], config.get(\"passing_reward_threshold\", 1.0)","numLines":216,"startLine":1,"totalLines":216}}}

\"\"\"\n    Compute the approximate KL divergence between two distributions.\n    Schulman blog: http://joschu.net/blog/kl-approx.html\n\n    Args:\n        log_probs: Log probabilities of the new    if kl_loss_type == \"kl\":\n        return log_ratio\n    elif kl_loss_type == \"k2\":\n        log_ratio = log_probs.float() - log_probs_base.float()\n        log_ratio = log_ratio**2 / 2.0\n        return log_ratio\n    elif kl_loss_type == \"k3\":\n        log_ratio = -log_ratio\n        log_ratio = log_ratio.exp() - 1 - log_ratio\n        return log_ratio\n    elif kl_loss_type == \"low_var_kl\":\n        # The non negative kl approximation in\n        # http://joschu.net/blog/kl-approx.html\n        # Besides non negative, it is also unbiased and have lower variance.\n        log_ratio = -log_ratio\n        log_ratio = log_ratio.exp() - 1 - log_ratio\n        return torch.clamp(log_ratio, min=-10, max=10)\n    else:\n        raise ValueError(f\"Unknown kl_loss_type: {kl_loss_type}\")\n\n\n@torch.compile(dynamic=True)\ndef compute_policy_loss(\n    ppo_kl: torch.Tensor,\n    advantages: torch.Tensor,\n    eps_clip: float,\n    eps_clip_high: float,\n    eps_clip_c: Optional[float] = None,\n):\n    ratio = (-ppo_kl).exp()\n    pg_losses1 = -ratio * advantages\n    pg_losses2 = -ratio.clamp(1 - eps_clip, 1 + eps_clip_high) * advantages\n    clip_pg_losses1 = torch.maximum(pg_losses1, pg_losses2)\n    clipfrac = torch.gt(pg_losses2, pg_losses1).float()\n\n    if eps_clip_c is not None:\n        assert (\n            eps_clip_c > 1.0\n The held-out eval tests 128 OTHER environments. For MaxDifferentGroupPairDivision, the scorer returns [-1, +1] where -1 is invalid/worst and +1 is optimal.\n\nBut the reward function has a potential issue. sum_sq) // 2\n        return S\n\n    def scorer(self, output: str) -> float:\n        \"\"\"Score the model output in [-1.0, +1.0].\n        \n        -1.0 : invalid output\n        +1.0 : optimal S achieved\n        Linear interpolation between worst_S (-1) and optimal_S (+1).\n        \"\"\"\n        processed = self.processor(output)\n        if processed is None:\n            return -1.0\n\n        S = self._compute_S(processed)\n        if S is None:\n            return -1.0\n\n        optimal_S = self.parameter[\"optimal_S\"]\n        worst_S = self.parameter[\"worst_S\"]\n\n        if optimal_S == worst_S:\n            # All partitions give the same S; any valid answer is optimal\n            return 1.0\n\n        # Map [worst_S, optimal_S] to [-1, 1]\n        score = 2.0 * (S -
